Runtime background WebP note
Files ending in -bg.webp are optimized derivatives of the credited -bg.jpg source files in this document. The same author, license, and source metadata apply to the WebP derivatives.

Milos photo candidates kept for attribution/history
The app only renders verified exact Milos beach photos. Nearby-coast or representative images are not used as beach-card/detail photos.
